估 3Q26 稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17% 
研華預估 3Q26 營收高於第二季，主因半導體、醫療、機器人、能源等需求
強勁，毛利率及營業利益率與第二季相當， 我們原估第三季營收季增 3%至
270 億元，年增 52%，由於 7~8 月合計營收 217 億元，達成率 80%，進度
超前，因此上修 3Q26 營收 17%至 316 億元，季增 21%，年增 78%，營收
向上，估毛利率略季增 0.2 百分點至 37.9%，稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，
年增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17%，主因營收優於預期。 
北美市場訂單強勁，為未來成長重心之一 
統計至 8 月研華整體訂單出貨比(B／B 值)為 1.47，美國市場則衝上 2.15，
公司預估到今年底仍可在 2 左右，顯示訂單需求強勁。另外，研華北美公司
到 8 月底營收年增率約 52%，上半年營收約 4.32 億美元，占集團整體營收
比重約 29%。美國是研華最大單一市場，也是今年增長最為強勁的市場之一。
由美國專案設計匯入（Design-Win）金額來看，1H26 已超過 4 億美元，高
於 去 年 全 年 水 準 ， 預 估2H26 可 接 近 甚 至 超 過 上 半 年 水 準， 今 年 美 國
Design-Win 業績應可達去年兩倍以上，美國將成未來成長重心。 
北美訂單強勁，上修 2026/27 年稅後 10%/13% 
研華北美訂單強勁，包括 半導體、醫療、機器人等業績強勁，邊緣 AI 逐漸
進入推廣及成長期，加上終端產品漲價，上修研華 2026/27 年營收 9%/12%
至 1,101/1,276 億元，成長 55%/16%，營業利益率 19.6%/20.6%，比 2025
年 16.3%明顯上升，主因規模經濟向上，費用率下滑。合計稅後 189/226 億
元，年增 78%/20%，EPS 21.75/26.01 元，比原先預期上修 10%/13%。研
華 1Q/2Q BB ratio 1.77/1.44 ，7、8 月份 BB ratio 仍達 1.64、1.47，其中北
美地區已至 2 以上，接單動能強大，預估下半年 BB ratio 維持高檔 1.4~1.5，
帶動業績保持高檔不墜，維持買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
估 3Q26 稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，年增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修
17%，主因上修營收 17%。 
⧫  
美國訂單強勁，專案設計匯入(Design-Win)金額來看，1H26 已超過 4
億美元，高於去年全年水準，預估 2H26 可接近甚至超過上半年水準。 
⧫  
北美訂單及 邊緣 AI 需求強勁 ，維持 買進 評等 。目標價 870 元係以
2H26~1H27 EPS 24.82 元、35 倍本益比推得。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第2 頁，共 23頁
營運分析
估 3Q26 稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，年增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17%，主
因上修營收 17%
研華 1Q25/2Q25/3Q25/4Q25 B/B ratio 為 1.08/1.08/1.01/1.33，1Q26 B/B ratio 大增至 1.77，主因
半導體設備、自動化、機器人等終端客戶需求強勁，且 DDR4/SSD 等關鍵零組件供應吃緊、價格
持續攀升，使客戶積極下單至未來 18 個月的長單以確保產能與料件。 2Q26 B/B ratio 維持高檔
1.44，其中北美/歐洲/中國三大區域分別為 1.92/1.25/1.21，其中半導體、自動化設備、通路、博奕
等接單仍強勁。目前看到 7、8 月 B/B ratio 上升至 1.64、1.47，預期 2H26 B/B ratio 1.4~1.5 區間，
尤其北美地區 8 月 B/B ratio 衝上 2.15，顯示公司訂單暢旺，帶動業績可望維持高檔。

研華預估 3Q26 營收高於第二季，主因半導體、醫療、機器人、能源等需求強勁，毛利率及營業利
益率與第二季相當，我們原估第三季營收季增 3%至 270 億元，年增 52%，由於 7~8 月合計營收
217 億元，達成率 80%，進度超前，因此上修 3Q26 營收 17%至 316 億元，季增 21%，年增 78%，
營收向上，估毛利率略季增 0.2 百分點至 37.9%，營業利益 64.2 億元，季增 25%，年增 137%。
業外受惠利息收入，投資收益、股息收入等挹注，獲利 5.7 億元。稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，年
增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17%，主因營收優於預期。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第3 頁，共 23頁  
圖 2：研華 2Q26 B/B ratio 1.44，客戶下單積極

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
北美市場訂單強勁 
統計至 8 月研華整體訂單出貨比(B／B 值)為 1.47，美國市場則衝上 2.15，公司預估到今年底仍可
在 2 左右，顯示訂單需求強勁。另外，研華北美公司到 8 月底營收年增率約 52%，上半年營收約
4.32 億美元，占集團整體營收比重約 29%。美國是研華最大單一市場，也是今年增長最為強勁的
市場之一。由美國專案設計匯入（Design-Win）金額來看，2025 年為 4 億美元，2026 年上半年已
超過去年全年成績，今年下半年可能接近甚至超過上半年水準，今年美國 Design-Win 業績應可達
去年兩倍以上，展現美國將成未來成長重心。北美市場 醫療、半導體及機器人成長最快，醫療為目
前最大應用，其中美國前 30 大醫療製造商，經過長期耕耘，研華已獲取 28 家，成績突出。 
半導體領域則因資料中心建置加快，以及 近年相關廠商積極擴產，強勢帶動研華北美業績。 針對水
冷散熱，研華未製造水冷硬體，而是提供 Edge Server 、HMI 與 I/O 設備給水冷廠商進行整合與液
漏偵測。另外公司還切入涵蓋電力、能源管理與 OT 維運，使半導體營運加快。在快速增長下，預
計研華北美半導體領域比重逐漸逼近醫療業務。 
機器人方面，業務範圍包括：機器手臂、 AMR(自主移動機器人)、協作型、人型機器人 等，目前機
器人部分營收最大為 AMR、協作型，現有增加無人機貢獻營收（如智慧農業）。人形機器人現在
都是 POC，主要來自中國、美國，韓國 ，大部分為新創公司，主要向研華買硬體，之後做成演算
法賣給大公司。機器人相關產業案子由去年的 130 幾個到現在 150 幾個，快速成長中。 
最後公司將戰略核心定調於深耕 AI 第五層的工業應用層，透過推出 IWS 一體機切入製造、能源與
醫療等行業，大幅降低企業導入邊緣 AI 的門檻，帶動整體基本面與長線發展穩健向上。IWS 有點
像目前 GB 10，只是等級更高，有望為研華創造新業績。

估 4Q26 稅後 54.8 億元，季減 2%，年增 77%，EPS 6.31 元，上修 17%，主因
營收優於預期，上調 16%
由於北美訂單強勁，即使 4Q26 有年底盤點因素，12 月業績略受影響，估營收持續季增 1%至 320
億元，年增 79%。毛利率在原物料仍處高檔情況，約維持第三季水準 38.1%。營業利益 62.6 億元，
季減 2%，年增 123%。業外受惠利息收入，投資收益、股息收入等挹注，獲利 3.8 億元。稅後
54.8 億元，季減 2%，年增 77%，EPS 6.31 元，上修 17%，主因營收優於預期，上調 16%。

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估、Bloomberg
受惠規模經濟上揚，營業利益率上升
研華高達 70%~75%業績來自零售與自有品牌(Channel Sales) ，公司保有高度訂價主導權，但零組
件漲價太大時，仍無法全部轉嫁出去，即使順利漲價，時間仍會略微落後；另外 25%的 ODM 業務
仍需面對較大價格競爭，漲價壓力較大。
上半年不僅記憶體大漲， CPU、PCB 等原物料亦加入漲價行列，雖然研華將終端產品陸續漲價反
應，且有遲滯風險。研華 2Q26 毛利率季減 1.5 百分點至 37.7%，低於預期，但 2Q26 營收季增
28%，年增 47%，受惠規模經濟上揚，費用率季減 2.8 百分點，營業利益率 19.6%，季增 1.3 百分
點，優於預期。在原物料維持高檔情況下，未來幾季毛利率將在 37~38%。2027 年原物料價格下
滑，毛利率可望上揚，因此估 2026/27 年毛利率 38.1%/38.6%，比 2025 年 39.8%略降，但營業利
益率 19.6%/20.6%，則比 2025 年 16.3%明顯上升，主因規模經濟向上。

邊緣 AI 占比持續上揚，有助維持長期毛利率
AI 訓練經過三年後，2025 年企業端及消費端 AI 推論需求上揚，加上 DeepSeek 等低價、開源 AI
模型推動，有助邊緣 AI 銷售提升。研調機構預估全球邊緣 AI 2025 -2032 年產值 CAGR 高達
23.8%。研華邊緣 AI 目前主攻六大產業：半導體、醫療、自動化、能源、機器人、零售。未來邊
緣 AI 將由選配變成標配及 AI PC 滲透率提高，推展速度增快。邊緣 AI 2023/24/25 年貢獻營收
4%/9%/18%，1H26 上升至 22.5%，研華估 2026 年底目標 30%，全年平均 25%，成長加快。公
司預期邊緣 AI 軟硬體結合策略，比公司平均毛利率高 2~3%，有助抵消上游原物料上漲負面因素。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第6 頁，共 23頁
現金股利增加
公司宣佈自 2026 年起取消每三年配發股票股利的慣例，改採全現金配息；經常性配息率由 70-
75%提升至 70-80%；並於未來三年(2026~2028 年)從資本公積每年加發特別股利 NT$2 元，2025
年合計配息 NT$11.2 元，配發率達 91%。預期 2026/27 年在資本公積每年加發特別股利 NT$2 元
下，配發率仍可達 85%左右。
北美訂單強勁，上修 2026/27 年稅後 10%/13%
研華北美訂單強勁，包括 半導體、醫療、機器人等業績強勁，邊緣 AI 逐漸進入推廣及成長期，加
上終端產品漲價，上修研華 2026/27 年營收 9%/12%至 1,101/1,276 億元，成長 55%/16%，上游
原物料大漲，雖公司終端產品轉嫁因應，但難以全移轉且有遲滯風險，估 2026/27 年毛利率
38.1%/38.6%，比 2025 年 39.8%略降，但營業利益率 19.6%/20.6%，則比 2025 年 16.3%明顯上

升，主因規模經濟向上，費用率下滑。合計稅後 189/226 億元，年增 78%/20%，EPS 21.75/26.01
元，比原先預期上修 10%/13%，主因上調營收。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第7 頁，共 23頁
產品結構分析
產品線含括各應用領域
研華為全球工業電腦 (IPC)與物聯網龍頭，其產品與核心業務主要劃分為以下五大事業群結構分析：
1)物聯網自動化事業群 (IoT Automation Sector)
• 核心定位：聚焦於「工業 4.0」與「智慧製造」的數據採集與實體控制層。1H26 營收占比 7%，
產品毛利率高達 65~70%。
• 產品結構：包含遠端遙控 I/O 模組(如經典的 ADAM 系列)、工業分布式資料擷取卡(DAQ)、工業
通訊網閘(Gateway)與自動化控制器(UNO 系列)。
• 市場趨勢： 結合研華自主開發的 WebAccess、EdgeLink 等工業軟體平台，提供工廠、能源與環
控系統從端點到雲端的完整數據串接。 受 惠 全 球 自 動 化 需 求 提 升 ， 估2026/27 年營收年增
49%/20%。
2)智能系統事業群 (Intelligent Systems Sector)

• 核心定位： 提供高效能、高擴充性的運算平台，作為大型基礎設施或邊緣 AI 應用的高階核心大
腦。1H26 營收占比 37%，產品毛利率 40~45%。
• 產品結構： 機架式工業電腦主機、智慧型機箱、網路安全通訊伺服器、影像應用電腦系統，以及
近年爆發的自主移動機器人(AMR)與無人載具(AS&R)控制系統。
• 市場趨勢： 隨著邊緣 AI 與實體 AI(Physical AI )需求大爆發，該事業群在北美半導體設備、網安、
醫療大單的專案導入帶動下，成為近年增速最為強勁的營收引擎 ，估 2026/27 年營收年增
63%/12%。
3)嵌入式事業群 (Embedded IoT Group / Embedded Sector)
• 核心定位：研華營收占比最大的核心支柱(1H26 占比約 35%)，全球市佔第一的工業級嵌入式板卡
供應商，產品毛利率可達 30~35%。
• 產 品 結 構 ： 包 含 工 業 級 主 機 板(Mini-ITX 、 Micro ATX ) 、 嵌 入 式 電 腦 模 組(COM-Express 、

SMARC)、工控儲存與記憶體解決方案、工業顯示器系統。
• 市場趨勢：近年主力朝向  Edge AI (邊緣人工智慧 )與 NVIDIA、Intel 等巨頭合作，推出搭載
GPU/NPU 的邊緣運算加速模組。 近期受惠醫療、半導體、工廠自動化、能源等產業出貨動能強勁，
估 2026/27 年營收年增 52%/17%。
4)智能服務事業群 (Intelligent Service Sector)
• 核心定位： 擺脫傳統工廠製造框架，將工業電腦技術延伸至「智慧生活與服務業應用」的垂直場
域。1H26 營收占比 6%，產品毛利率 35~40%。
• 產品結構： 數位電子看板、智慧零售 POS/POI 終端、車載行動電腦與物流車隊管理系統、高階
醫療用電腦與數位手術室一體化系統。
• 市場趨勢： 主要針對智能醫療、智慧零售、智慧物流等特定行業（Vertical Markets）提供軟硬整
合解決方案。2026 年因通膨及戰爭影響，零售出貨疲弱，估營收年減 7%，2027 年因基期變低，
實體 AI 需求逐漸上揚，估營收年增 22%。

5)Advantech Service+ / Others 
• 核心定位： 負責全球後勤、DMS(設計製造服務)、全球在地化組裝與客製化加值服務。 1H26 營
收占比 15%，產品毛利率 20~25%。 
• 產品與服務結構： 提供結合硬體的 BIOS 客製化、作業系統(OS)整合；並包含營運代管服務、客
戶售後全球運籌物流(全球 4 大運籌與 21 個在地服務中心)。 
• 市場趨勢： 在「地緣政治與供應鏈重組」浪潮下，此項目透過在美、歐、日等在地化組裝與透明
化 BABA/非中國零件物料分析，協助提高研華設計專案（Design-Win）的爭取能力。北美 Design-
Win 大增，加上地緣政治與供應鏈重組浪潮下，估 2026/27 年營收年增 105%/18%。

同業比較分析  
研華工業電腦主機板市占率領先同業 
研華為全球工業電腦龍頭，產品涵蓋工業主機板、嵌入式系統、工業自動化及邊緣運算等領域，並
以自有品牌及全球通路布局為主要競爭優勢。公司約 70~75% 業績來自零售與自有品牌（Channel 
Sales），具高度訂價主導權，且全球銷售與服務據點完整，有利於掌握不同產業客戶需求及維持
長期客戶關係。 
與主要同業相比，研華產品布局較為完整，2025 年營收以 Embedded/iSystems 為主，占比分別為 
36%/34%，產品由工業主機板、嵌入式模組延伸至完整工業系統、自動化及垂直應用解決方案 ；
工業電腦主機板市占率達 12.4%，領先全球。排名第二為 Kontron(已被樺漢併入)，主攻歐洲市場，
除工業電腦硬體外，亦涵蓋軟體、交通及航太國防等高附加價值解決方案。東擎產品組合相對集中
於工業主機板、嵌入式系統及  Edge AIoT 平台。研華憑藉較完整產品組合、自有品牌及全球通路
布局， 2025 年營收  708.8 億元，規模明顯高於主要同業，營業利益率及淨利率分別達  
16.3%/14.9%，展現規模經濟及營運效率優勢。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第10 頁，共 23頁
客戶分析
研華終端應用客戶分散
研華作為全球工業電腦 (IPC) 與邊緣 AI (Edge AI) 的龍頭大廠，核心戰略從早期的「硬體元件供應」
全面轉型為「硬軟整合平台（如  IWS 平台）與邊緣運算方案提供者」。以下針對研華指定的七大
終端應用客戶領域進行分析：
1)自動化製造(智慧工廠)：占營收比重約 30~35%
• 市場痛點與需求：傳統產線面臨缺工、設備老化、製程盲點，以及跨國工廠數據無法即時量化的
瓶頸。
• 研華解決方案：提供工業級主機板、無風扇工業電腦、以及結合視覺檢測的邊緣  AI 智能系統。
• 客戶應用效益：協助客戶落實預測性維護 (Predictive Maintenance) 與 AI 視覺瑕疵檢測，讓產線
稼動率與良率全面被數據化提升，並能將成功案例快速複製到全球工廠。
2)醫療(智慧醫療)：占營收比重約 15~20%
• 市場痛點與需求：醫療器材（如手術機器人、高階影像、導航系統）要求高精準度、低延遲、且

必須通過各國極其嚴格的醫療級法規認證。
• 研華解決方案：開發醫療專用電腦 (Medical PC)、手術室整合控制核心以及邊緣 AI 醫療影像板卡。
• 客戶應用效益：在高階智慧醫療器械的滲透率極高，以北美市場為例，前  30 大醫療設備製造商
中就有 28 家是研華客戶，穩居核心供應商地位。
3)能源(智慧能源與環境)：占營收比重 10~15%
• 市場痛點與需求：各國企業與政府面臨迫切的  ESG 減碳規範 、高電價壓力，以及分散式電網
（太陽能、風力、儲能）的調配難題。
• 研華解決方案：推出智慧能源管理系統 (iEMS)與環境監控資料採集器。
• 客戶應用效益：幫助客戶即時監控碳足跡與用電能耗，優化儲能系統配置，達到永續低碳轉型的
法規要求。
4)智慧城市(智慧零售與基礎建設)：占營收比重 10~12%
• 市場痛點與需求：現代都市需要更高效的公共基礎設施、無人化自助服務、以及大量公共數據的
邊緣端處理能力。
• 研華解決方案：智慧 POS 系統、自助服務機 (Kiosk/UTK)、一體式電腦 (UTC) 以及影像 AI 運算

平台。
• 客戶應用效益：大幅減少零售門市與公共場所的人力成本，提升無人化交易體驗與城市基建的運
作效率。
5)資安(邊緣安全與信賴供應鏈)：占營收比重 10~12%
• 市場痛點與需求：當大量設備連接至邊緣端 (Edge)，網路攻擊風險大增；此外，美中地緣政治使
得敏感產業（如半導體、國防）對「非中國零件 (Non-China Component)」要求極高。
• 研華解決方案：研華從硬體底層植入資安晶片，並提供最完整、透明的物料清單  (BOM) 分析及替
代方案。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第11 頁，共 23頁  
• 客戶應用效益：透過符合美國 BABA (Build America, Buy America) 規範的在地生產鏈，確保高機
密性客戶（如半導體前/後段製程設備商）免於供應鏈中斷與資安漏洞的風險。 
6)交通 (智慧交通)：占營收比重 10~12% 
• 市場痛點與需求：鐵路、捷運及大型車隊需要適應極端震動與溫差環境的系統，以進行即時票務
處理、車隊監控與安全防護。 
• 研華解決方案：部署於捷運讀卡機、自動售票機、車載硬體，並整合多攝影機系統（ Multi-
camera Systems）與車載邊緣運算。 
• 客戶應用效益：提供大眾運輸系統高度穩定的核心硬體，實現大數據流量的即時運算與精準車隊
車況調度。 
7)物流 (智慧物流)：占營收比重 8~10% 
• 市場痛點與需求：電商爆發帶來倉儲空間與 驗貨人力的雙重吃緊，冷鏈與貴重資產在運輸途中極
易損壞失聯。 
• 研華解決方案：工業級機器人控制器、自動化倉儲系統，以及最新推出的太陽能戶外資產追蹤方
案。 
• 客戶應用效益：為無人搬運車 (AGV) 與自主移動機器人 (AMR) 提供大腦（如結合 NVIDIA/Intel/
高通平台的運算單元），有效優化倉儲調度、追蹤跨國資產狀態。

財務分析  
資產負債表健全，存貨上升具實質需求支撐 
資產負債表部分，研華流動比、速動比 1Q26、2Q26 出現下滑，落在 170%/100%上下，仍屬可接
受水平，負債比率落在 26%~42%健康區間。營運現金流量穩定維持正數，顯見公司資金控管得當。
另外，營運現金流近年維持在 80~107 億之間，預期 2027 年將大幅提升至 201 億元，資本支出於
2025 年達 28.4 億元，預期 2026、2027 年略減少至 21.6/19.4 億元。ROE ( 股東權益報酬率)研華
維持在 17~32%區間，屬於健康水準。 
研華 4Q25~2Q26 存貨週轉天數由 95.7 天提升至 103.9 天，應收帳款天數由 53.2 天降至 51.1 天，
應付帳款天數則由 62.4 天延長至 70.4 天，收款改善及付款天數延長部分抵銷備貨的資金壓力。同
期存貨金額由 118.3 億元增至 209.1 億元，2Q26 年增 92.3%，高於單季營收約 47%的增幅。存貨
增加主要受零組件漲價及業務成長備貨影響，由於公司銷售佳，客戶訂單供不應求，目前無影響。

產業概況  
工業電腦未來 CAGR 6%，為下游成長穩定的產業 
工業電腦在 2000 年後網路發達，亦造成工業電腦用途日漸擴大，由工廠發展至生活端。過去常受
全球製造業景氣影響，後因應用面變廣，成長性加快。 
工業電腦近年成長率在 5~10%，是下游成長穩定的產業。根據研調機構統計及預估，工業電腦
2023 年產值約為 45 億美元，可望增加至 2037 年的 96 億美元，CAGR 6% 比一般 PC 低個位數成
長為佳，主因公司對工業物聯網、製造業數位化、邊緣 AI 建置…等因素推動，其中半導體、製造
業、汽車、物流、零售…等均為重要成長來源。 
圖 17：工業電腦 2023-2037 年產值 CAGR 6%

資料來源：Markets and Markets、元大投顧整理 
工業 4.0 帶動物聯網需求大增 
從 18 世紀末經歷多次工業生產變革，20 世紀末進入 21 世紀，工業製造由 3.0 發展至 4.0，其中最
大改變就是組裝、加工、搬運 …等由原先自動化機台 (工業 3.0)，加入智能回饋，透過物聯網收集
巨量資料，傳輸至雲端運算，統計分析後，即時改善生產製程。隨時因應機台老舊、材料或周遭環
境變化，自動調整量產系統。 
第四次工業革命和過去相比，網路無所不在，移動性提高；感測器體積變得更小、性能更強大、成
本也更低。與此同時，人工智慧和機器學習也開始嶄露鋒芒，相關應用不斷增加。

### Page 14

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第14 頁，共 23頁  
圖 18：歷次工業變革

資料來源：元大投顧整理 
預估人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)2025~2034 年 CAGR 達 22.16% 
物聯網基礎架構分為五層：物件、通訊與網路、運算與儲存、應用與服務、分析與統計。台灣工業
電腦公司較專注在前端物件 (感測元件)以及中端的運算與儲存二項業務。尤其前端的感測元件為台
灣強項，諸如：POS 機、ATM、機器人手臂、安控攝影機…等。近年受惠人工智慧(AI)導入，成長
加快。 
全球人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)未來受惠於 Data Center、AI Server 建置、企業數位轉型…等因素穩定
成長，研調機構預估 AIoT 2025~2034 年 CAGR 22.16%，為未來工業電腦成長重心。人工智慧物
聯網(AIoT)在全球使用廣泛，目前工業電腦產業使用方面已經陸續切入智慧家庭、車聯網、自動化
設備、機器人…等領域。

式控制的自動化設備提高生產
效率、品質。
德國在漢諾威工業博覽會提出
工業4.0的概念。導入物聯
網、巨量資料、雲端服務，對
生產服務進行分析、決策、
控制。
定位 機械化 電氣化 自動化 聯網化

上修 2026/27 年稅後 10%/13%
受到記憶體大漲，研華終端產品漲價反應，且邊緣 AI 需求強勁，上修研華 2026/27 年營 收
9%/12%至 1,101/1,276 億元，成長 55%/16%，上游原物料大漲，雖公司終端產品轉嫁因應，但難
以全移轉且有遲滯風險，估 2026/27 年毛利率 38.1%/38.6%，比 2025 年 39.8%略降。合計稅後
189/226 億元，年增 78%/20%，EPS 21.75/26.01 元，比原先預期上修 10%/13%，主因上調營收。

景氣處於上升階段，預估下半年 BB ratio 維持高檔 1.4~1.5。邊緣 AI 需求上揚，應用面：半導體、
醫療、自動化測試設備、機器人等在各區域業績強勁，帶動下半年業績高檔不墜。 維持先前目標本
益比 35 倍，以 2H26~1H27EPS 24.82 元計算，目標價 870 元，潛在上漲空間 19.8%。

公司簡介  
研華(2395 TT) 為全球第一大工業電腦嵌入式板卡製造商，全球市占率約 27%。主要業務為各項工業電腦解決方案，如：零
售、交通、國防、基建、醫療、綠能 …等。產品組合方面 (2025)，iAutomation 7% 、iSystems 34% 、Embedded 36% 、
iService 11% 、AS+ 12% ；銷售區域(2025)北美 31%，歐洲 16%，中國 21%，北亞 9%，台灣 7%，其他亞洲及新興市場
10%，其他地區 6%。主要生產地區包括：大陸昆山廠、台灣林口廠及日本北九州廠。

### Page 20

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第20 頁，共 23頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 研華整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先的
位置，在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名也領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 研華的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略優於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含人力資源、數據隱私與網路安全、商業道德等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：研華針對重要 ESG 議題的整體管理能力很強，公司已建立董事會對 ESG 議題的監
督機制，其 ESG 報告部分符合最佳實務標準。公司有完善的人才資本發展計畫，包括人才招募、培育和留任的相關措
施。然而，研華在商業道德以及賄賂和貪腐風險管理上的計畫較弱。此外，管理系統已通過 ISO 27001 認證。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

EQUITY: HANDSETS
Largan Precision   3008.TW 3008 TT
Global Markets Research
5 October 2026
Highly sensitive to CPO FAU growth
CPO on track, likely to contribute c.18% of 2028F 
earnings; but near-term Apple demand could be lighter
Action: Maintain Buy; TP raised to TWD8,300, implying ~23% upside
We reiterate Buy on Largan, and raise our TP to TWD8,300, based on 30x 2028F EPS of 
TWD276.7 (previously: TWD6,000, on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD240.0). We cut our earnings 
forecasts by 7.2% and 10.4% for 2026F and 2027F, respectively, to reflect the adverse 
impact of rising BOM costs on smartphone demand and potential cannibalization of the 18 
Pro series by the iPhone Duo. For the first time we incorporate the co-packaged optics fiber 
array unit (CPO FAU) revenue into our earnings model, by assuming the new business 
would contribute 1%/18% of 2027/28F earnings. Hence, our 2028F earnings imply 44% 
y-y growth, driven by CPO FAU and Apple’s (AAPL US, Not rated) camera upgrades. We 
raise our target P/E from 25x to 30x to reflect Largan’s smooth progress in CPO FAU and a 
higher sensitivity to its earnings. Every one million units of CPO FAU could contribute 
c.TWD10 EPS to Largan, in our calculation. The target P/E of 30x exceeds the high end of 
the stock’s historical trading band (10-25x) since 2015. Largan trades at 35x 2027F P/E.
CPO FA/FAU progress on track
As we highlighted in June  and July , Largan can offer a full-range of CPO FAU components 
and modules, including prism/microlens array (PMLA), FA, and FAU, and has the strength to 
fully automate the FA and FAU assembly process by leveraging its strong internal machine 
tool capability. By doing so, Largan’s time-to-market and cost structures for FA and FAU 
assembly should be competitive vs peers. We estimate the total CPO FAU market could 
grow from less than 5mn units in 2027F to 30-40mn units in 2028F, and we expect 
Largan’s market share to be at 10%/15% in 2027/28F (Fig. 1 ). We assume the ASP to be 
over USD100, as its major customer will likely prefer Largan to handle FA/FAU assembly 
rather than just supply components. We believe its fully-automated pilot run line was 
ready by end-3Q26, and the first MP line could be ready by 1H27F. 
Apple: Sunny’s market share loss and continued camera upgrades in 2027-28F
Sunny (2382 HK, Neutral) earlier faced some yield issues with the new variable aperture (VA) 
main camera lens for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and this could benefit Largan and Genius 
(3406 TT, Buy). However, given the headwinds of rising memory costs and the potential 
cannibalization of the 18 Pro series by the iPhone Duo, we see slower iPhone lens shipments 
from end-2026F to 1H27F, before the 20th anniversary iPhone (Pro Max) and the second-gen 
iPhone Duo are introduced in 2H27F. We expect a further upgrade in iPhone Pro’s main 
camera in 2H27F, and introduction of a new periscope camera for the Pro Max in 2H28F.

Company profile
Largan is a technology leader in handset camera lenses, and is currently Top 2 plastic lens maker in the world.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD8,300 is based on 30x 2028F EPS of TWD276.7. Our target P/E of 30x exceeds the high end of the stock’s historical trading band
(10-25x) since 2015. The benchmark index for the stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price
Downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected sales of iPhones and Android phones; 2) level of inventory correction by Chinese customers; 3) higher-

than-expected ASP/margin erosion; 4) slower-than-expected spec upgrades; 5) increased competition, causing market share loss; and 6) worse-
than-expected production yields.
ESG
Largan focuses on resource recycling and voluntarily reported green house gas emission through attending ISO14064 in 2020. Largan has achieved
90%+ water recycling and targets to save 1% electricity every year.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, subject to 
limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an appropriate 
valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow analysis, expected 
return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated target price, defined as 
Nomura | Largan Precision 5 October 2026
7

### Page 8

(target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that the 
analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 'N/A' are 
not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging Markets (ex-Asia): 
MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and by 
other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

穩懋 (3105 TT)  Buy

PIN 等光通訊訂單效應持續推升業績向上，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：穩懋 08/2026 營收 19.37 億元， MoM+2.40% ，符合
群益先前預估， 3Q26 營收動能來自 optical 、 infrastructure ，預
估 09/2026 營收仍將較 08/2026 增加。就評價而言，穩懋受惠於
PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產，預估出貨量 4Q26 將
持續放大，且後續成長空間可期，為 4Q26 、2027 年重要營收動
能，故群益維持對穩懋 Buy 的投資建議。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 QoQ+83.22% ： 2Q26 營收 52.57 億元，
QoQ+14.54% ，動能來自 cellular 、infrastructure 、optical 等產品，
此外，雖然 optical 新產品量產初期良率較低，不過，由於 2Q26
產品結構優於 1Q26 、產能利用率提高，毛利率提高至 28.24% ，
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+72.14% ，加上認列 1.44 億元匯兌利益、
2.70 億元投資利益， 2Q26 稅後淨利增加至 9.77 億元，
QoQ+83.22% 。  
就 2Q26 的產品結構而言， cellular 產品營收 QoQ+18.00% ，原
因在於手機業者需求隨逐漸進入旺季而增溫； infrastructure 產
品營收 QoQ+14.53% ，動能來自 AI data center 、低軌道衛星；  
optical 產品 2Q26 重要動能有二， (1) 新款 iPhone 相關 VCSEL
訂單數量增加， (2)PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產。  
PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大為後續重要營收動能： 展望
3Q26 ， optical 產品受惠於 PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大，營
收成長性居各產品之冠，預估將帶動 others 產品營收
QoQ+35.97% ，且後續成長空間仍可期；其次， infrastructure 產
品出貨動能來自低軌道衛星標案陸續出貨，預估相關營收
QoQ+20.45% ，由於全球各地低軌道衛星標案隨應用領域拓展而
有機會持續釋出，後續營收前景亦仍佳。  
至於 cellular 、 Wi-Fi 產品出貨成長性雖受到近期記憶體缺料等
因素影響整體手機產業景氣而存在不確定性，但現階段預估營
收均與 2Q26 實績相去有限。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，穩懋持續透過各項節能措
施提升廠內能源使用效率，以減少用電度數，且新設產線全面採用最佳可行
技術，實際措施包括 PFCs 尾氣處理設備全面採用去除效率達 90% 以上之型
式 (IPCC 公告指定型式 )、逐步汰換既設產線之 PFCs 尾氣處理設備、評估製
程氣體減量使用或取代高潛勢溫室氣體使用、採取工程改善或設備更換以
降低用電量、評估新建廠房設置廠內再生能源等。  
再就企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果而言，穩懋致力於新技
術的開發，持續推出高效能的技術平台，以生產更具能源效益和節能的半導
體通訊產業產品，因終端產品應用逐漸擴及手機、無線網路、雷達、衛星等
各個通訊領域，有助於全球能源節省和減少用電消耗。此外，藉由穩懋多功
能的晶片整合、製程線寬的縮減以及製程能力的提升，終端產品之尺寸、單
位晶片使用量均持續減少，亦得以為守護地球環境盡一份心力。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

由於北美 Honda 新車型訂單貢獻顯著，帶動 3Q26 營收淡季不淡，加上
電子新業務的訂單開始貢獻，為豐祥下半年營運挹注強勁成長動能， 研
究部據此上修豐祥今年獲利 8%，預估2026 年 EPS 17.17 元(+41%YOY)。
展望 2027 年，預期在休閒車輛、電動機車新訂單以及電子新業務貢獻
下，豐祥明年業績將繼續攀高，預估 2027 年 EPS 21.09 元。研究部給
予 12 倍本益比，將目標價上調至 250 元，維持買進評等。

重點摘要：

1. 電子業務啟動第二成長曲線，2027年進入量產階段： 
豐祥將電子業務視為企業新一輪發展的核心驅動力 ， 當前以AI
伺服器機櫃為主力產品以及其他電子相關品項。 今年下半年已
陸續有新產品少量出貨。在營收比重部分，2026年電子業務約
占合併營收的3~5%。未來隨著客戶需求釋放及新項目量產，搭
配新廠房啟用，創造更多生產空間，公司規畫2H27開始以大規
模自動化模式提升產能， 預期2027年電子業務將有更多的營收
貢獻，更能增添中長期營運動能。。

2. 3Q26淡季不淡，4Q26營收再攀高，年度獲利上修： 
豐祥第三季繼續受惠北美Honda新專案出貨順暢，季度營收 表
現優於原先季減的預期， 呈現淡季不淡 ， 延續前季的強勁表現 ，
年增率保持大幅成長 ； 之後進入越南當地電動機車零件拉貨旺
季，第四季營收有望再度衝高。整體而言，在休閒車輛與電動
機車業務雙引擎帶動下，公司下半年營收動能無虞，本業獲利
維持在高檔區域並保有顯著的年成長動能 ， 加上轉投資事業獲
利成長，研究部上修豐祥今年度獲利9%，預估豐祥2026年合併
營收94.68億元(+39%YOY)，毛利率23.4%(+1.0pct)，費用率
9.4%(+0.8pct)， 預估稅後淨利11.94億元(+41%YOY)， 預估2026
年EPS 17.17元。

3. 三大成長引擎接棒，2027年業績將再創高： 
展望2027年，研究部認為豐祥將繼續保有偏強的營運動能，主
要來自於三大核心領域 ：(1)休閒車輛新訂單持續堆疊與放量 。
特別是受惠於北美供應鏈轉移效益 ， 豐祥對客戶的供應滲透率
提升，北美Honda、Harley-Davidson等客戶陸續有新機種將於
明年量產；亦有日系客戶的新訂單將提前至明年出貨，使休閒
車業務在2027年依然是貢獻整體營收增長的最主要基石；

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

(2)越南當地電動機車與電動汽車市場規模擴大，相關訂單持
續增加，為既有業務提供成長支撐 ；(3)電子新業務的實質貢
獻。豐祥切入伺服器機櫃供應鏈，經過2026年下半年的試產準
備後，2027年將迎來訂單放量，預期電子業務佔營收比重躍升
至5%以上。此外，休閒車輛占比提高，產品組合將繼續優化，
毛利率能再墊高；配合物流逐漸回歸自營管理，費用率亦有望
下降，將進一步提升盈利能力。研究部預估豐祥2027年合併營
收116.46 億元(+23%YOY) ，毛 利 率23.7%(+0.3pct) ，費 用 率
9.1%(-0.3pct)， 預估稅後淨利14.70億元(+23%YOY)， 預估2027
年EPS 21.09元。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
6667 信紘科 (TRUSVAL)

報告日期： 2026/10/05            研究員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 訂單滿載與高能見度：信紘科現有訂單達 230 億元，受惠於
AI 與半導體擴建潮，廠務設備與二次配工程訂單滿載，能見度
看至 2028 年。  
 大型統包與海外擴張：業務從單一工程升級至數十億級大型機
電統包，並結合策略夥伴進軍美、日、德、新等海外市場，帶
動營收成長。  
 綠色永續與持續收益：成立專責子公司銳源環境科技，聚焦廢
液處理與資源循環，將一次性工程建置延伸至維修與代操等持
續性收入。

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二.半導體擴建為主要成長動能，工程與統包範疇擴大 
在廠務與工程服務部分，信紘科目前量產與工程實績包含特氣特
化廠務供應設備、系統建置及二次配管線裝機工程。為進一步提
升單案規模與營運效能，公司將服務領域延伸至二次配統包與大
型機電統包（GC/EPC），使專案金額邁入數十億級規模。後續業
務擴張重點，亦包含綠色製造與循環經濟，於 2025 年 12 月成
立專責子公司「銳源環境科技」，聚焦廢液處理與資源循環，將
一次性建置工程延伸至維修、營運代操及再生產品銷售等多元商
業模式等長期持續性收入。

目前主要服務據點皆配合客戶全球擴產需求建置，公司在台灣擁
有苗栗、台中、台南等據點，憑藉完整的本土供應鏈與人力調度，
能就近支援各大晶圓廠新建、擴建與改建之即時需求（主系統工
投資評等：買進

      20x 2027 年 EPS

程週期約 6 至 8 個月，二次配約 4 至 6 個月）。在海外市場
方面，信紘科已設立日本、美國及泰國子公司，並積極結合策略
聯盟夥伴切入德國與新加坡等新興市場，帶動海外營收比重持續
提升(今年約 10%)。受惠全球 AI 與先進製程/先進封裝擴產潮，
全球預估有近 20 座晶圓廠同步施工，且先進製程新建廠之工程
規模約為成熟製程的 4 至 5 倍，公司可參與的工程範圍從主系
統、二次配及部分廠務工程，進一步擴大至海外及機電統包等大
型工程，將為公司帶來長期龐大之承攬動能。

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後
純益 7.57 億元，稅後 EPS 14.76 元。展望 2027 年，受惠全球
半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 330 元 
綜合考量 (1)先進製程相關半導體需求維持強勁 (2) 公司可參與
的工程範圍擴大至海外及機電統包等大型工程，並觀察歷史及同
業本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 16 倍評價偏低 ， 故投資建議為「買進」，
目標價 330 元（20x 2027EPS）。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
信紘科技（Trusval Technology，股票代號：6667）成立於 1995 年，
深耕高科技製造供應鏈布局，並由既有廠務供應設備系統服務，
逐步拓展至機電統包、建廠總承攬及整合型工程解決方案。目前
業務範疇涵蓋廠務設備、製程二次配裝機服務，以及高科技廠房
整合建置與機電統包工程，可依客戶建廠及擴產需求，提供從工
程規劃、設計、採購、施工、測試驗證至系統整合與專案管理的
一站式服務。憑藉跨工種整合、精密製程系統建置及大型專案管
理能力，公司持續提升高附加價值工程案件比重，協助客戶縮短
建廠時程、確保工程品質並提升營運效率。因應全球淨零轉型與
資源循環需求，信紘科技亦積極發展製程機能水設備、廢液處理
與循環經濟解方、資源零廢中心建置等業務，將工程整合能力延
伸至綠色製造與永續基礎建設。

台灣為公司最核心且成熟的基本盤，憑藉超過 30 年高科技產物
工程的深厚經驗，累積了卓越的技術能力與執行實力；得益於台
灣完整的高標準供應鏈以及敏捷的人力與材料調度，能高效支援
客戶新建、擴建及改建廠需求，加上工程需求穩定且執行週期較
短（主系統約 6 至 8 個月，二次配約 4 至 6 個月），構築了
公司長期穩健的營業基礎。

為打造未來成長新引擎，公司順應客戶海外建廠的全球布局與緊
湊時程，將成熟的工程能力輸出海外，信紘科不僅以既有產物工
程為切入點，逐步擴展至純水、廢水、無塵室及機電等領域以擴
大單一案件服務範疇，在非美日等新興區域更結合策略夥伴共同
承攬；同時，公司統包能力持續升級，從早期高技術門檻的二次
配工程，拓展至跨系統二次配統包，進而邁向承接大規模、高整
合度的跨國 GC 與工程管理角色，透過整合台外資源提升接單層
級與案件金額，全面貼近客戶全球建廠需求。

二.產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
A.半導體產業概況  
半導體產業為全球數位經濟之核心基礎建設，近年在人工智慧
（AI）、高效能運算（HPC）及邊緣運算應用加速普及帶動下，產
業需求結構出現顯著變化。先進製程與高密度運算晶片需求持續
提升，使晶圓廠投資動能自庫存調整週期中快速復甦，並進入新
一輪資本支出擴張階段。 根據國際半導體產業協會（SEMI）2025
年發布之預測，全球 300mm 晶圓廠設備支出於 2026 年至 2028 年
間累計將達約 3,740 億美元，其中 2025 年單年度支出將首次突破
1,000 億美元水準。顯示全球半導體建廠投資已由短期循環性波
動，逐步轉為結構性成長態勢。另依 Deloitte（2025）預估，全球
半導體銷售額有機會於 2030 年前後突破 1 兆美元規模，反映產業
長期需求動能仍具成長潛力。

B.半導體產業 ESG 趨勢 
隨淨零排放與國際永續規範逐步落實，ESG 已由合規要求轉為半
導體供應鏈的重要競爭要素。依 SNS Insider（ 2025）預估，全球
半導體廢液處理市場將由 2024 年的 27.2 億美元成長至 2032 年的
49.2 億美元，其中亞太地區受惠於晶圓廠持續擴建與永續轉型，
成長動能可望高於全球平均。在此趨勢下，半導體廠務環保技術
逐步朝 「資源再生、能源轉化、源頭減量」三大方向發展。首先，
硫酸、氨氮廢水及有機溶劑等製程廢液，正由傳統末端處理轉向
回收再生，經純化後重新作為工業級或電子級原料使用，提高資
源循環率並降低廢棄物處置需求。其次，有機廢液處理逐步導入
Waste-to-Energy 技術，將廢液轉化為燃料或能源使用， 以降低外購
能源需求與碳排放。

此外，先進製程亦更加重視源頭減量，透過綠色化學品替代與先
進流體製程，降低強酸、強鹼及水資源使用量。隨 3nm 以下製程
持續發展，兼具降低化學品耗用、廢水負荷與營運成本效益的技
術，導入需求可望提升。 
整體而言，未來半導體廠務市場將由單純設備供應，逐步轉向整

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二、預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後
純益 7.57 億元，稅後 EPS 14.76 元。展望 2027 年，受惠全球
半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

Document 190: Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

13023135     1

Economy 
Entering the age of the older consumer 
01 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• The older consumer is moving center stage. America's population has been aging for decades, but the shift has accelerated: the 
share aged over 60 increased by almost 10 percentage points (pp) between 1995 and 2025. And the Census Bureau projects the 
share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population. 
• More free time means a different spending mix. People aged 65 and over spend around two and a half fewer hours per day 
working and roughly two hours more on leisure than the overall adult population. Bank of America data shows that older 
households allocate relatively more of their card spending to groceries and travel, and less to restaurants, gasoline, general 
merchandise and clothing. 
• Households aged over 55 held close to $140 trillion in net worth in the second quarter of 2026, giving many older consumers 
considerable capacity to spend on travel and other leisure categories. But wealth is not evenly distributed, and healthcare and 
other cost pressures remain significant for older households on tighter or fixed incomes.

Population aging is nothing new, but it is accelerating  
The US population has been growing relatively “older” for a long time. Exhibit 1 shows that the proportion of the population 
under 20 years old shrank from the mid-1960s through 2025. At the same time, the proportion of the population in older age 
groups has been rising.  
Exhibit 1: Over time the US population has steadily aged  
Proportion of US population by age range (%)

Source: Human Mortality Database; Bank of America Institute calculations 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

### Page 2

2 01 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Additionally, there has been a notable acceleration in the relative aging of the population in recent years. For example, between 
1995 and 2025, the proportion of the population aged over 60 rose by almost 10 percentage points (pp) to 25%.  
Several factors have driven this. First, fertility rates in the US, as in much of the world, have fallen sharply. This has slowed 
natural population growth and, by reducing the number of younger people relative to older cohorts, has increased the share of 
the population at older ages. Second, more recently, net inward migration to the US has slowed. Because migrants tend to be 
younger than the overall population, weaker immigration also tends to raise the average age. Finally, rising life expectancy over 
much of the post-war period has allowed more people to survive into older age, further increasing the older population's share.  
Exhibit 2: The trend towards an older composition of the population is expected to continue  
Forecast changes in the share of population by age group relative to 2025 (pp)

Source: United States Census Bureau 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Lower fertility and, more recently, weaker net migration look set to slow overall US population growth in the decades ahead. As a 
result, older Americans are likely to play an increasingly important role in the economy, both as a share of the population and, in 
many age groups, in absolute numbers. The Census Bureau projects the share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 
2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population (Exhibit 2). For businesses, understanding how the needs and spending patterns 
of older consumers differ from those of younger generations will therefore become increasingly relevant. 
Older age groups allocate their time differently  
Why does this matter? One reason is that older people tend to spend their time and money quite differently from younger adults. 
Most obviously, many work less or have retired altogether. Data from the American Time Use Survey (ATUS) shows that people 
aged 65 and over spend, on average, around two and a half fewer hours per day working than the population aged 15+ (Exhibit 
3). 
That leaves considerably more time for other activities. People aged 65 and over spend around two hours more per day on 
leisure and sports activities, with television viewing accounting for a particularly large share of this additional leisure time 
(Exhibit 4).

Older households also spend differently  
Bank of America internal data on credit and debit card spending provides insights into how these differences in older 
generations’ use of time also impact their spending. Rather than splitting our spending data by generation, as we often do, here 
we split by age bands, allowing for consistent comparisons over time.  
Exhibit 5 shows how the composition of card spending of households aged 61-75 and those older than 75 differs from that of all 
households. A few things stand out. For one, older households devote a greater share of their overall card spending to groceries 
(food and beverages). Households aged 61-75 also allocate more spending to travel (airlines and lodging). And restaurants and 
bars, gasoline, general merchandise and clothing account for a smaller share of older households’ card spending.  
These differences may seem fairly intuitive. Older households have more leisure hours, so spend relatively more on travel to 
enjoy them. Previously we found that more available time may support travel particularly around retirement, although the 
discretionary spending lift can be temporary (for more, read our publication: Paychecks to pensions: The evolution of retiree 
spending).  
Older households are also likely to have less need to update their work wardrobes, so they probably spend less on clothing.

Exhibit 5: Older households allocate more of their card spending to groceries and travel, but less to eating out, gasoline, general merchandise and

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Still, older households’ relatively lower spending on restaurants and bars is a little more curious, perhaps, given dining out could 
be one way of making use of more leisure time. Indeed, when we look at how these spending shares are evolving, we find a 
nuanced picture. While households aged 61-75 do spend relatively less on restaurants and bars than the overall population, they 
appear to be narrowing this gap. But for those older than 75 this is not the case. In our view, the pandemic may still be weighing 
on dining-out spending among this older age group (Exhibit 6).  
Exhibit 6: For restaurants and bars the story is mixed across older

of all households, although it remains below its 2019 level (Exhibit 7).

Aging presents opportunities, but also challenges 
In our view, the aging of the US population presents important opportunities for the economy and businesses. Older consumers 
have more leisure time and businesses that cater to these demands may therefore benefit. 
Moreover, some older consumers hold considerable amounts of net wealth and have been some of the biggest beneficiaries of 
recent rises in equities. Using data from the Federal Reserve, Exhibit 8 shows that as of the second quarter of 2026 the net 
worth of those households aged 55 and over was close to $140 trillion, around three-quarters of the total. And with equity 
markets rising strongly, these age groups have seen a more than 20% increase in their net worth over the past two years, 
potentially enhancing their spending power further.  
But an important caveat is that not all older consumers are in this position. Previous Institute work found evidence consistent 
with equity-market wealth effects being concentrated toward higher-income households (read our publication: Paychecks to 
pensions: The evolution of retiree spending). And Census Bureau research has found that around 14% of Social Security 
recipients aged over 65 rely on it for over 90% of their income. For older households without a wealth cushion, recent elevated 
cost pressures, like gasoline, will likely have put them under more significant financial strain. They will also be more dependent 
on cost-of-living (COLA) increases in Social Security to underpin their spending.  
And, more broadly, outside of card spending, healthcare costs are a much larger factor in older households’ budgets (Exhibit 9). 
The evolution of these costs going forward will be a key variable for older consumers’ budgets.  
Exhibit 8: A large and growing proportion of net worth is held by 
older cohorts

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts.  
Durables spending is defined as spending on electronics, building materials, auto-related spending and furniture. Premium durables spending is 
based on a selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively higher value products. Conversely, value durables spending is based on a 
selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively lower value products.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Executive summary

台灣半導體暨科技產業公布 9 ⽉份及 3Q26 營收，我們以公布的數據看產業趨勢
變化及公司與彭博社分析師預期進⾏分析⽐較，投資⼈須審慎評估。總⽽⾔
之， 3Q26 營收明顯超過彭博社分析師預期的公司⼤幅多於低於預期的公司達 6
倍多，⽽超過 53% 年增（⽤晶圓代⼯的 9 ⽉份營收年增做標竿）的有 6 個次產
業。除此以外，我們還提供了 3Q26 營收年增明顯改善及惡化的前 20 名公司整
理及排序，及 3Q26 營收明顯季增 / 季減的前 20 名公司整理及排序。總結⽽
⾔，伺服器產業鏈（對標公司有 Nvidia, AMD, Intel, ⾕歌 TPU ， Dell,
SuperMicro) ，存儲記憶體（對標公司有三星 , SK Hynix, SanDisk,
Western Digital) ， CCL/PCB ，半導體建廠及測試產業 3Q26 營收（對標公司
有  Teradyne, Advantest, Form Factor ）明顯⾼於分析師預期， 3Q26 營收
年增改善明顯的有存儲記憶體，光纖通訊（對標公司有 Lumentum,

Fabrinet, Coherent ）， CoWoS/CoPoS 先進封測設備產業。 3Q26 營收季增
明顯改善的產業有 CoWoS/CoPoS 先進封測設備產業，設計服務（對標公司有
Broadcom, Marvell) ，存儲記憶體，光纖通訊，及伺服器產業鏈。

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

GIS-KY(6456) 
建議：維持中立 
結論：2026 年仍處於營運整理期

一、GIS-KY 觸控面板大廠，目前切入指紋辨識、近眼
光學業務 
GIS-KY 鴻海集團旗下觸控面板大廠，自 2011 年成立，
2012 年開始生產觸控模組 ， 成立之初是以貼合技術為核
心，結合專業的研發與製造，來提供客戶在人機介面的
整體解決方案。最大客戶為 Apple，近年除了將業務推
進至LCM 組裝外 ， 也積極發展指紋辨識 、 近眼光學業務。
2025 年主要產品營收占比，平板 61%、筆電 19%、指
紋辨識 15%、其他(車載+工控)5%。

二、2Q26 本業持續虧損，EPS-3.15 元 
GIS 2Q26 營收 105.02 億元，年減 44.8%，毛利率則從
前期 5%降至 3.3%，主要是因記憶體缺料使筆電及平板
無法拉貨導致營收大幅下滑，致使本業續虧。此外，因
母公司有資金與償還銀行借款需求，海外子公司盈餘匯
回使稅務費用提列，稅後淨損為 10.5 億元，EPS-3.15
元。 
三、未來業務主軸由人機介面轉向半導體光學 
展望下半年 ，GIS-KY 表示營收將呈現季季增的態勢 。 主
要業務觸控及顯示業務較為保守，指紋辨識業務穩定，
車載 、 光學及其他較樂觀 。 投顧預期3Q26 營收年減 17%，
主要是受記憶體短缺使終端客戶拉貨力道不足的影響，
估計 3Q26 稅後 EPS -0.1 元。整體而言，預期 2026 年
平板及 NB 業務佔比降至 70%左右，車載及指紋辨識則
約為 20%，光學及其他則有機會上升至 10%左右。 
GIS 表示未來策略重心將從傳統的人機介面，轉向「半
導體光學」(Semi-optics)，即利用半導體等級製程來製
作奈米級光學元件。其中，1)Micro-LED：用於 AR 眼鏡
的顯示，並具備短距光通訊潛力；2)SRG 光波導 
(Waveguide)：用於 AR，已在台中后里建置產線；3)車
用 AR-HUD：利用光波導技術縮小體積；4)光學鏡片：
Metalens、Pancake Lens 等，鏡頭用於高階 VR。光學
業務於 2026 年雖有專案開始量產，但營收貢獻仍小，
預計 AR 與 VR 相關應用要到 2027 年底至 2028 年，才
會開始有較顯著的營收貢獻。另外，光通訊業務主要聚
焦光源封裝，包括 CW Laser(連續波雷射)等產品，並同
步評估 Micro LED 於短距離光傳輸的應用可能性，公司
已接到美系 Coherent 的 CPO 光耦合元件 （800G/1.6T)
大單，主要應用於 AI 資料中心的高速數據傳輸，預料
光源封裝業務將在 26 年底有初步出貨，27 年上半年開
始陸續放量。為加速推動半導體光學轉型策略，GIS 今
年度資本支出預估約 80~90 億元。該筆資金將主要投
入光波導與微型發光二極體等新光學產品設備，其中有
約 40~50 億元主要用於光通訊雷射光源封裝 、 測試相關
設備。海外產能布局方面，去年 Q3 動工的越南新廠預
計於今年底完工，並力拚年底前通過客戶驗證進入量產
階段。 
四、維持中立理由 
投顧認為 GIS 目前仍以 NB 及平板觸控業務為主，隨記
憶體短缺使終端需求仍疲弱 ， 預期2026 年營收年減 15%，
營運表現未有提升，2027 年才會有明顯躍升的機會，預
期 2026 年 EPS -3.25 元，2027 年 EPS 3.28 元，維持
中立投資建議，目標價 80 元。

Style Exposure
We assume coverage of Jentech at OW with a Dec-27 PT at NT$7,500, based on
30x 2028E P/E. We are positive on the company’s near-term and long-term
earnings drivers including: (1) the adoption schedule of the 3-piece / removable lid
design (removable lid + stiffener + multiple screws) could accelerate to 4Q26 with
small production in Rubin GPU and mass production from 1Q27 for Rubin/Rubin

Ultra, lifting lid content ~4–5x versus the current 1-piece design; (2) AMD MI450-
series moves to a 2-piece lid from 4Q26, driving ~10x content versus MI350; (3)
longer-cycle upside comes from CPO/NPO ramps with higher stiffener content;
and (4) potential ASIC upgrades to higher-spec solutions. As Jentech’s share price
has rallied 108% in the past three months (vs. TWSE index +102%), we believe the
above-mentioned positive factors have started to be reflected in the share price,

making our PT upside less attractive (~10%) vs. other cooling component names.
• No delay in 3-piece/removable lid adoption in Rubin Ultra, even pulling
forward some volume in Rubin potentially from 4Q26/1Q27. We continue
to believe Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra GPU will adopt the “removable lid + stiffener
+ multiple screws” design (or the so-called 3-piece lid design) with no delay
or change, which should drive the heat spreader/lid content increase by 4-5x vs.

the current 1-piece lid design for Rubin GPU. Based on our industry checks,
Nvidia might even adopt this new design in small volumes of Rubin GPU
potentially from 4Q26 for trial production, in order to ensure decent yield when
entering mass production in the next gen (potentially in 1Q27). We believe the
adoption schedule is slightly ahead of market expectations, which might
provide near-term revenue/earnings upside for the 3-piece lid supplier.
• Solid 3Q26 and more growth into 4Q26 and 1Q27 with MI450 2-piece lid

ramp and initial removable lid contribution for Rubin/Rubin Ultra.  We
estimate our 3Q26 revenue at NT$9.7bn (+33% QoQ and +91% YoY) with the
decent ramp of Rubin GPU. As the shipment of Rubin 1-piece lid should
remain at high volume into 4Q26, we believe the company’s revenue
momentum will also continue, with incremental growth from the mass
production of AMD MI450 2-piece lid with ~10x content increase (vs. MI350
series). Hence, we are now modeling Jentech’s 4Q26 revenue to further grow

7% QoQ. In addition, if the 3-piece/removable lid design could be adopted in
small volume of Rubin GPU from 4Q26 and enter mass production from 1Q27
in Rubin Ultra GPU, we believe there could be further upside to the company’s
growth magnitude in the next few quarters.
• Valuation re-rating already kicking off. Jentech is now trading at ~50x 12M
fwd P/E, at the high-end of its historical valuation range, but from the 2028E
EPS-based perspective, it is only trading at 25-30x P/E. With a longer-term

Sources for: Style Exposure – J.P. Morgan Global Markets Strategy; all other tables are company data and J.P. Morgan estimates.

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation
Investment Thesis
Jentech (OW) is the largest packaging-level cooling component
maker worldwide, with leading production capabilities across
heat spreader products (including stiffener and all kinds of lids).
We expect the company to show an ~80% EPS CAGR over
2024-28, driven by solid heat spreader content growth in future-

gen AI chips and increasing TAM into new solutions (e.g., CPO
switch ASIC). In addition, the company is reinforcing its
product portfolio with new offerings in cold plate and CPU
server sockets for chip customers. We believe Jentech’s
technology leadership in the structural cooling upgrade trend
and its new TAM expansion will continue to support its superior
earnings power in the long term.
Valuation
Our Dec-27 PT of NT$7,500 is based on 30x 2028E P/E, which

is largely in line with Jentech’s three-year average P/E when the
AI server boom started.

Earnings estimates
JPM’s above-consensus forecasts
Our 2026E/27E/28E EPS forecasts are 1%/3%/6% above Bloomberg consensus,
reflecting 1) faster adoption of the removable lid design in 4Q26/1Q27 for Nvidia
Rubin/Rubin Ultra GPU with 4-5x content increase vs current 1-piece lid, 2) better heat
spreader spec upgrades in future GPUs and ASICs, and 3) more meaningful CPO/NPO

ramp with higher-spec solutions being adopted.

Key risks to our estimates
• Potential delay in mainstream 3-piece/removable lid design adoption: As the
spec/TDP of Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra GPU has not yet been finalized, there is still risk
of the 3-piece/removable lid adoption if the chip spec improvement turns out to be
limited. However, based on our recent industry checks, Nvidia might even adopt this
new design in small volume of Rubin GPU potentially from 4Q26 for trial
production, in order to ensure decent yield when entering mass production in the

next gen (potentially in 1Q27), which would lower the risk.
• Market share loss from competition: Based on our supply chain checks, another
Taiwanese heat spreader supplier, I-Chiun (2486 TT) has secured some CPU and
ASIC stiffener projects. However, in terms of the AI GPU lid and other higher-spec
projects (e.g CPO ASIC stiffeners), we think there is a huge gap between Jentech
and other suppliers.

Performance and valuation
Share price performance
Figure 1: Jentech’s share price vs Taiex
Source: Bloomberg Finance L.P.
• In 2025, Jentech’s share price underperformed in 1H given the market concerns of
an AI bubble and potential heat spreader de-specing trend (from lid to stiffener).
However, the share price outperformed from 2H given the new high-spec heat

spreader designs (such as micro-channel lid, 2-piece lid, etc) potentially for Nvidia’s
future AI GPUs to enable TDP migration.
• In 1Q26, Jentech’s share price continued outperforming the Taiex in the beginning
with new TAM expansion opportunities from Nvidia’s new solutions, especially the
CPO related ones (e.g. Spectrum and Quantum ASICs). From the mid of 2Q26, the
share price corrected meaningfully owing to the Rubin GPU design change from 2-

piece lid back to 1-piece lid with only ⅓ of ASP.
• In 3Q26, Jentech’s share price was still weak in the beginning among the overall
market pullback from de-leveraging. However, it has rebounded rapidly from
August thanks to 1) reported GM/EPS numbers beat, 2) smooth Rubin 1-piece lid
ramp with ASP upside, and 3) more meaningful content value growth for Rubin
Ultra GPU with 4-5x ASP vs the current 1-piece lid.

Financial analysis
We forecast Jentech’s revenue to increase 61%/95%/59% YoY in 2026E/27E/28E,
supported by the removable lid design for Nvidia Rubin/Rubin Ultra GPU with 4-5x
content increase (vs current 1-piece lid), with continued heat spreader spec upgrades in
future GPUs and ASICs. Also, the more meaningful CPO/NPO ramp with higher-spec

solutions adopted also contributed to the solid growth.
Jentech’s 2Q26 gross margin expanded to 47% thanks to continued QD yield
enhancement. We believe the company’s margin will continue to surprise to the upside
in 2H26-2028, driven by the favorable product mix from stronger AI server product
shipments, which should support our gross margin forecast of 47+%/50%/52% in
2H26E/2027E/2028E. The solid GM growth also serves as the key driver of our bottom-

line EPS growth of 89%/117%/68% in 2026E/27E/28E.

J P M O R G A N
Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
Jentech Precision Industrial Co. (Overweight; Price Target: NT
$7,500.00)
Investment Thesis
Jentech (OW) is the largest packaging-level cooling component maker worldwide, with
leading production capabilities across heat spreader products (including stiffener and all
kinds of lids). We expect the company to show a ~80% EPS CAGR over 2024-28, driven

by solid heat spreader content growth in future-gen AI chips and increasing TAM into new
solutions (e.g. CPO switch ASIC). In addition, the company is reinforcing its product
portfolio with new offerings in cold plate and CPU server sockets for chip customers. We
believe Jentech’s technology leadership in the structural cooling upgrade trend and its new
TAM expansion will continue to support its superior earnings power in the long term.
Valuation

Our Dec-27 PT of NT$7,500 is based on 30x 2028E P/E, which is largely in line with
Jentech’s three-year average P/E when the AI server boom started.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Downside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) Potential delay in mainstream 3-
piece/removable lid design adoption, and 2) market share loss from competition.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of Tommy Liou at Cathay Life Insurance Co., Ltd..

るだけに、FRBも経済や物価の反応を見極めるた
め、政策金利を現状維持
＜代替シナリオ②（インフレ圧力が早期に沈

静化）：利上げを停止（同15％）＞
 エネルギー価格の迅速な低下によって、供給要因
による国内物価への波及が迅速に収束
 需要主導のインフレ圧力は残存するが、2026年12
月の利上げによって4％台に達する政策金利によ
り十分引き締め的と判断して現状維持に転換
 資産価格のオーバーシュートとその後の急速な調
整によって、経済の下方リスクがむしろ増大
 基調的インフレのアンカーがポイント
＜メインシナリオ（基調的インフレ率が目標に
収斂）：政策金利の速やかな中立化（確率
60％）＞
 日銀は、9月利上げの際に、経済の底堅さに自信
を示す一方、基調的インフレの上振れリスクを意識
 植田総裁は、基調的インフレを2％近傍に収斂させ
ることを主眼とする「新たな局面」に入ったとの認識
 本年12月にさらに25bpの利上げを行い、年末には
1.50％。2027年3月にも追加利上げで政策金利は
1.75％へ到達
 ただし、政策金利が明確に中立化する中で、経済
活動への負荷が徐々に明確になる可能性

 日銀によるビハインドザカーブの懸念が後退する
中で、長期金利の焦点は財政運営（消費減税の財
源等）や米国の長期金利による影響へ
＜代替シナリオ①（インフレ圧力が想定以上
に継続）：利上げを継続（同25％）＞
 景気が底堅い下で、エネルギー価格の高止まりに
よる国内物価への波及が強まりつつ継続
 インフレ期待を抑制するため、FRBは2027年中に
利上げを継続し、2027年末に5％近傍へ上昇
 経済や物価への影響だけでなく、金融環境のタイ
ト化が非直線的に出現するリスク
＜代替シナリオ②（経済の回復が想定以上に
遅延）：利上げの減速（同10％）＞
 輸出の好調が内需に波及せず、経済活動が停滞
 円安の抑制も必要であるため、日銀はより緩やかな
ペースの利上げにシフト
 日米金利差の拡大による円安圧力が輸入インフレ
→実質購買力低下→一層の内需停滞を招くリスク
＜代替シナリオ①（エネルギー価格の高騰が
想定以上に継続）：金融引き締めの領域へ（同
30％）＞
 エネルギー価格の高止まり、ないし円安の継続に

よって国内物価への波及が想定以上に長く継続
 基調的インフレの上振れを阻止するため、日銀は利
上げを継続し、ターミナルレートは2％台前半に上昇
 経済と物価のトレードオフが深刻化し、政府の経済
政策との調整が困難化

日本経済見通し（宮嶋）
米国経済見通し（渡辺）
＜ポイント＞
 足下では台風や長雨等による悪天候や中東情
勢悪化によるコスト高が逆風の中 、AIブームによ
る米国経済の堅調さ 、実質賃金の緩やかな改善
（上図）等から、景気は底堅く推移
 米国経済の緩やかな回復が続く中 、食料品の消
費減税による押し 上げ効果等から、27～28年度
の景気は回復局面が継続 。29年度は減税効果
剥落を想定し てやや減速も、需給ギャップはプラ
ス圏で推移すると予想（下図）
＜リスク＞
 中東情勢の予想以上の悪化により 、原油高が一

段と進行すればコスト高の逆風が強まるリスク
 高市政権の積極財政による景気上振れ。ただし、
市場不安定化による金利高等の副作用の懸念も
＜ポイント＞
 米国経済は個人消費と 設備投資を牽引役に堅調持
続（上図）。個人消費は株高の資産効果が背景
 金利上昇で住宅市場は悪化。一方、潤沢なキャッ
シュフローを背景にAIデータセンター投資は活況
 足元の株式市場はAI企業の利益成長が主導する
「業績相場」と、金利上昇でPERが下がる「逆金融相
場」が併存。綱引きで株価には停滞感出るも、長期
的には名目GDPに沿って上昇継続の公算（下図）
 大統領2年目の株価は、中間選挙が与党不利と なり
やすく、政策停滞懸念から軟調傾向。3、4年目は大
統領選に向けた政策テコ入れで騰勢が強まる傾向
＜リスク＞
 金利急騰やAIブームの腰折れで株安と景気後退
 イラン紛争の長期化によるスタグフレーション
 インフレ急進とFRBの大幅利上げで景気後退

単位労働コストの抑制が基調物価を押し下げ

（単位労働コスト前年比、％）
（年）
（前年比、％）
基調的な物価
（コアPCEデフレータの景気循環部分、右軸）
単位労働コスト
（３四半期先行、左軸）
日本物価見通し（宮嶋）
米国物価見通し（渡辺）
＜ポイント＞
 関税や中東情勢に伴うエネルギー高によりコア財
物価が上振れ（上図）
 長期インフレ予想は安定しており、賃金・サービス
物価への二次的波及は限定的。エネルギー高が
ホームメード・インフレに発展するリスクは限定的
 労働生産性の上昇が単位労働コストを抑え 、基
調的な物価を抑制する見込み（下図）

 供給要因のインフレは27年前半に剥落へ。ただし、
堅調な米景気がディマンドプル ・イ ンフレを 招き、
27年以降も物価は2％目標に着地しない公算
＜リスク＞
 AI関連の需要増で電子部品や電力の価格を中心
にインフレ上振れ
 AIによる生産性向上のディスインフレ圧力
 イラン紛争早期終結で油価下落、インフレ下振れ
 トランプ大統領がFRBの独立性を脅かし、金融政
策の信認と物価抑制効果が低下する恐れ
 政府債務増大とドル価値の低下に伴う インフレ
＜ポイント＞
 現状の物価は政府の支援策により上値が抑えら
れている格好（上図）。同対応策の終了を想定す
れば、中東情勢悪化によるインフレ圧力により 、
コアCPIは26年度末に3％近傍に加速する公算
 今後の中東情勢の小康状態を想定し 、食料品の
消費減税による下押し効果もあり 、27年度のコア
CPIは減速に向かう と 予想。28年度以降は減税
効果剥落により 再び加速へ。減税効果を除けば 、
物価は2％目標圏内で推移すると予想

 長期インフレ予想は足下 2％程度（下図）。今後
は中東情勢悪化による影響剥落や物価目標達
成への信認から目標圏で推移と予想
＜リスク＞
 原油高や財政悪化懸念、それに伴う 円安が再度
進めば物価上振れリスク大
 食料品の消費減税に関わる政策の詳細が不透
明で、物価への影響も 不確実性が大きい
注：コアCPIは生鮮食品除くベース
インフレ率は2027年以降も2％目標に着地しない公算
出所：BEA、FRB、SFGI

（政策金利、％）

政策金利（左軸）

（右軸）
市場予想
（左軸）
（期待政策金利、％）
日本の金利見通し（宮嶋）
米国の金利見通し（渡辺）
＜ポイント＞
 日銀はインフレ上振れ防止のため利上げをペー
スアップ 。先行き は、12月・来年3月に利上げ予
想（金融政策の頁参照） 。その後の政策金利は
基調物価の2％前後の推移の下で横ばいを想定
 長期金利は 、利上げペースアップ等から 上昇に
歯止め。ただし 、米金利高や日銀国債保有減少
等から、3％近傍の高水準継続と予想（上図）
 超長期金利は世界的な財政懸念等が逆風も、発

行減額等への期待から小幅に低下へ （下図）
＜リスク＞
 想定以上のイ ンフレ加速と なれば、政策金利が
2％を超え、イールドカーブ上方シフトのリスク
 グローバルな財政懸念や高市政権の日銀利上
げ牽制姿勢が強まれば 、長・超長期ゾーンの金
利上昇が加速（スティープ化）
＜ポイント＞
 長期金利＝期待政策金利（政策金利予想）＋ター
ムプレミアム（国債保有リスク）（上図）
 タームプレミアムの上昇要因は、トランプ政策を巡
る 不確実性から、財政不安や国債の需給悪化懸
念、中間選挙後の不透明感へシフト。AI企業の大
量起債に伴う投資家資本の争奪戦も一因
 期待政策金利が上昇。イ ラ ン 紛 争 に 伴 うコ ス ト プ ッ
シュ・インフレと 堅調な経済指標がFRBを タカ 派化さ
せ、市場の利上げ期待を押し上げる（下図）
＜リスク＞
 イラン紛争早期終結で原油安、金利低下
 インフレや財政悪化、トランプ氏のFRBに対する政
治圧力等による「悪い金利上昇」
 ウォ ーシュFRB議長のフォワードガイダンス曖昧化

で、金融市場のボラティリティが上昇
 FRBの資産圧縮の加速でタームプレミアム急上昇
 金利上昇による景気後退の結果、金利下振れ
米国：長期金利＝期待政策金利＋タームプレミアム
注：期待政策金利とタームプレミアムはNY連銀のACMモデルの推計値

Document 265: Record-breaking tanker rates pile pressure on already high fuel prices

Source PDF: all report pdf/Record-breaking tanker rates pile pressure on already high fuel prices.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Crude tanker rates have hit all-time highs amid the prolonged
Iran war and Strait of Hormuz disruption. Restructured oil ﬂows
and a rush for capacity to handle oil transport operating under
military protection have driven rates sharply higher, adding
further pressure to fuel prices alongside strong reﬁnery margins
Rico Luman
Record-breaking tanker rates along with operating in war zones and elevated
insurance premiums are all contributing to high fuel prices

Tanker shipping thrives in risky waters amid
a continued capacity squeeze
Oil tanker markets sit at the centre of geopolitical turmoil, and this
year that has translated into record-breaking freight rates as the
conﬂict in the Middle East drags on. Tankers have been caught up in
sanctions regimes and repeatedly targeted in key shipping lanes,
including the Strait of Hormuz, Bab al-Mandeb and the Gulf of Aden.
At the same time, demand for tanker capacity has surged as

traders seek alternative routes and sources of supply, including

### Page 2

increased shipments from the US. The opening of a protected
corridor through Omani waters has enabled additional crude
exports and vessel movements, but has also placed further strain
on an already stretched market. Shuttle services now operate
around the Strait of Hormuz, with under the radar activity and ship-
to-ship transfers taking place outside the chokepoint.
The conﬂict and tanker seizures in the Persian Gulf erupted at a
time when the market was already facing a shortage of capacity.

New deliveries of crude carriers slowed sharply following a record-
low order book in 2022-23, while an increasing number of older and
smaller vessels joined Russia's shadow ﬂeet, further reducing the
pool of compliant tankers available to the wider market.

transport costs

Source: Clarksons, ING Research
Crude tanker earnings rose tenfold on rush
for capacity
The capacity squeeze, combined with operating in risky war zones

and facing highly elevated insurance premiums, has sent tanker
rates soaring. We have seen previous surges in this volatile market,

### Page 3

such as in 2022 after sanctions were placed on Russia, but this
spike is beyond levels ever seen before.
Average global crude vessel earnings exceeded an unprecedented
level of $500,000 per day, 10 times the 2025 average, in early
October. For Suezmax tankers and very large crude carriers (VLCCs,
with a maximum capacity of 2m barrels) alone, rates surged even
beyond that. Crude shipments from Ras Tanura in Saudi Arabia to
Rotterdam made up about $2/bbl in 2025; in September, this

surged beyond $35. Combined with high reﬁnery margins (the
current crack spread of diesel in Europe is about 2.5 times the 2025
average), this could add over $0.50 to the base price of a litre of
diesel at the gas station.
Earnings of product tankers have also tripled compared to the 2025
average. Nevertheless, these have responded much more
moderately. This is mainly because of the key importance of the
Middle East region for crude oil. And when it comes to the

supertankers, the VLCCs, these are designated to transport crude
and traditionally are intensively deployed on the Middle East trade
routes.

on a bullish market

### Page 4

Source: Clarksons, ING Research
Crude tanker orders pick up after a
prolonged downturn
Tanker markets are cyclical by nature, and there’s a clear
connection with freight rates. Over the pandemic years of 2020-21,
tanker earnings hit lows due to lower oil consumption and
shipments. This resulted in an order book of just 4% of global crude
carrier capacity, the lowest on record since the 1990s.
With the recovery of oil demand and tanker rates in 2022 and

2023, order intake began to rebound. This year, in particular, order
books have doubled from 14% to 28%. Although the average age
of the crude ﬂeet has also reached a multi-decade high of over 13
years and more scrapping can be expected, we can also expect
more supply pressure. But many of these vessels will only be
delivered in 2028-29, likely after the current boom ends. It’s all
about geopolitics and how the Middle East conﬂict evolves, but
tanker markets could still see a solid 2027.

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

The end of the safety net:  
Life without the macroeconomic stabilisers

1 
Authors 
Henry Allen 
Macro Strategist

28 September 2026 
For several decades, financial markets and the wider economy benefited from an implicit 
safety net. If a slowdown or a downturn arrived, there was a monetary put from central 
banks, and a fiscal put from governments. The monetary put would see interest rates 
slashed and liquidity injected via QE. The fiscal put would see governments run larger 
deficits to cushion any downturn. These policy stabilisers smoothed out the business 
cycle, and supported asset valuations too. 
These policy interventions were increasingly normalised from the late-1980s, and became 
more aggressive with each passing cycle. This reached its peak during the Covid-19 
pandemic, when governments ran fiscal deficits unprecedented in peacetime, whilst 
many central banks expanded their balance sheets with huge asset purchases. 
But after an era of ever-bigger interventions, these old stabilisers now face major 
obstacles. On the fiscal side, policy is constrained today by sovereign yields at multi-year 
highs, alongside the highest debt-to-GDP ratios in several decades. Then on the monetary 
side, policy is constrained by persistent inflation that’s increasingly supply-driven. 
This represents a big shift from the last few decades, but it does echo previous regimes 
where the macroeconomic stabilisers were either blunted or left unused. A good example 
is the 1970s experience, when stagflation and supply shocks posed a dilemma to 
traditional policy tools. Even further back, the pre-WWI environment of the classical gold 
standard and balanced budget orthodoxy saw governments play a very different and more 
limited role in macroeconomic stabilisation. 
Looking forward, this environment means policymakers will have a much more difficult 
time stabilising the business cycle in the coming years. So absent a sharp fall in yields or a 
collapse in inflation, higher macro volatility will be here to stay.

### Page 2

28 September 2026
Thematic Research
Page 2 Deutsche Bank AG
1. Background: How the stabilisers used to work.
Economic policymakers have long used interventions to stabilise output. This
became increasingly fashionable after WWII, when Keynesian economics
established a more active use for fiscal policy to stimulate the economy in a
downturn. This meant budget deficits became a more regular feature of life, rather
than used solely for wartime emergencies.

It was from the late 1980s, however, that the extent of policy interventions became
increasingly sizeable. Indeed, it was here that the origins of a central bank “put” for
financial markets began, in the crisis of Black Monday in 1987. That was a huge
stock market crash, where the S&P 500 fell over 20% in a single day, marking the
biggest daily decline in its entire history. The size of the falls even went beyond the
Great Depression, raising fears of wider contagion, particularly if it were to spark a

liquidity crisis that caused financial institutions to fail. However, the Federal
Reserve responded by cutting rates and providing liquidity, offering a
macroeconomic stabiliser against the prospect of wider economic stress. This
offered support against a wider downturn, and the US economic expansion
continued until 1990.
Whilst the genesis of a central bank “put” came in 1987, the idea gained further
traction in the late-1990s, with the Asian financial crisis in 1997, followed by the

Russian financial crisis in 1998. Fears of an economic contraction mounted,
particularly after issues at Long-Term Capital Management (LTCM) raised concerns
that the US wasn’t immune from these global pressures. So once again, the Fed
delivered three 25bp rate cuts pre-emptively, seeking to support the economy.
A few years later, the bursting of the dot com bubble in early 2000 set the stage for
yet further stimulus. As equity markets declined, the Fed began to cut rates from

early 2001, ultimately delivering 475bps of cuts that year that took rates from 6.5%
to just 1.75%. That was followed up by further rate cuts in 2002 and 2003, leaving
rates at 1% by mid-2003 into 2004. Moreover, the Bush tax cuts were also delivered
in 2001 and 2003, acting as a fiscal stimulus as the wider US economy entered
recession.
In hindsight, these moves seem modest compared to what then happened in 2008,
when the Global Financial Crisis led to even bigger policy interventions. Suddenly,

the world faced the biggest economic contraction since the Great Depression, with
genuine fears that a repeat might happen. To avoid this, policymakers launched into
the biggest economic stimulus ever seen in a peacetime environment. That
included massive fiscal stimulus, with the US federal deficit at 9.8% of GDP in 2008,
the highest since 1945. Moreover, central banks slashed rates to the zero lower
bound, and many like the Federal Reserve launched into Quantitative Easing

programmes, purchasing assets to ease financial conditions even more. Over the
coming years, many began pledging to keep rates low well into the future,
pioneering the concept of forward guidance.
These levels of policy intervention weren’t confined to the immediate crisis
however. In the subsequent years, active attempts were made to prolong and
extend the business cycle, particularly as the 2010s became an era of low inflation
and low interest rates. In this environment, borrowing was historically cheap, and

low inflation meant central banks could cut rates without seeing a big overshoot of
their targets. Indeed, both the Fed and the ECB undershot their 2% inflation target
on average in the 2010s, and the ECB took their own deposit rate into negative
territory in 2014.

By 2019, the year before the pandemic, the intellectual climate had continued to
move in the direction of more policy intervention. Government bond yields hit new

lows, with Germany’s 10-year bund yield at -0.7% in summer 2019. This
environment of negative yields meant borrowing was basically a “free lunch”, and
that summer also saw the Fed begin a 75bp easing cycle, as inflation was below
their target and they wanted to sustain the expansion.
The peak of this policy interventionism was seen with the Covid-19 pandemic in
2020. Just as in 2008, the world was facing a huge and sudden economic
contraction. Moreover, the consensus among policymakers was that they could

have done more to speed up the recovery after 2008, potentially avoiding years of
very high unemployment. As such, 2020 saw the most aggressive economic
stimulus packages ever seen in a peacetime environment, well above those in 2008.
Central banks immediately slashed rates back to the zero lower bound, pledged to
keep rates low for years to come, and launched into major QE programmes again.
Then on the fiscal side, record peacetime deficits were run. Indeed, the US federal

deficit of 14.5% of GDP in 2020 was the highest since 1945.

Page 4 Deutsche Bank AG
During the pandemic period, many initially expected these interventions to remain
for years to come. After all, in mid-2020, financial markets continued to price a
continuation of the low inflation and low interest rates of the 2010s. This belief was
clear on several fronts, and the 10-year Treasury yield closed at just 0.51% in August
2020. Meanwhile, the Federal Reserve even adjusted its own framework to allow for

average inflation targeting, i.e. permitting an overshoot of the inflation target after
periods of an undershoot. This reflected the consensus view that inflation was
unlikely to be a serious threat.
But as we now know, inflation returned in a serious way after the pandemic, forcing
central banks into rate hikes. Meanwhile, public debt-to-GDP ratios rose
significantly thanks to the pandemic stimulus, helping to push up sovereign bond
yields too. In other words, many of the usual policy tools of the previous few

decades were either blunted or no longer available.
2. The Monetary Constraint: High inflation means the central
bank put isn’t available
In the pre-2020 world described above, central banks regularly grappled with how
to generate enough inflation. In part, this was thanks to several structural forces
that put downward pressure on global prices. For instance, deepening
globalisation acted as a positive supply shock that kept costs down. Meanwhile, the

expansion of the global labour force in these years also boosted the supply side. In
other words, inflation was low and central banks generally had the space to cut
interest rates when they needed to.
But since the pandemic, we have entered a very different environment of above-
target inflation across many of the world’s biggest economies. Initially, that was
caused by a huge wave of pent-up demand after the pandemic, alongside
disruption to supply chains and labour markets. But the subsequent years also

brought further shocks. Those included Russia’s invasion of Ukraine in early 2022,
driving a big spike in European energy prices, and pushing Euro Area inflation above
10% briefly. In 2026, the closure of the Strait of Hormuz then acted as a fresh energy
shock, pushing inflation further away from target and contributing to a fresh
tightening cycle from the major central banks.
